# 19.시편 (EXB)



## 1장

**1:1** Book 1: Two Ways to Live ·Happy [Blessed] are those who don’t ·listen to [walk in the counsel of] the wicked, who don’t ·go where sinners go [stand in the way of sinners], who don’t ·do what evil people do [sit in the seat of mockers].  
**1:2** They ·love [delight in] the Lord’s ·teachings [laws; instructions], and they ·think about [meditate on] those ·teachings [laws; instructions] day and night.  
**1:3** They are like a tree planted by ·a river [streams of water; full of life, strong, vibrant]. The tree produces fruit in season, and its leaves don’t ·die [wither]. Everything they do will ·succeed [prosper].  
**1:4** But wicked people are not like that. They are like chaff that the wind blows away [dead, unstable].  
**1:5** So the wicked will not ·escape God’s punishment [stand in the judgment]. Sinners will not ·worship with God’s people [be in the assembly of the righteous].  
**1:6** This is because the Lord ·takes care of his people [knows the way of the righteous], but the way of the wicked will be destroyed.  

## 2장

**2:1** The Lord’s Chosen King Why ·are the nations so angry [do the nations rage/or conspire]? Why ·are the people making useless plans [do the people plot in vain]?  
**2:2** The kings of the earth ·prepare to fight [take their stand], and their leaders ·make plans [plot] together against the Lord and his ·appointed one [anointed; Messiah; the king, ultimately Jesus].  
**2:3** They say, “Let’s break ·the chains that hold us back [their chains/bonds] and throw off ·the ropes that tie us down [their ropes/cords from us].”  
**2:4** But the one who sits in heaven [God] laughs; the Lord ·makes fun of [ridicules; derides] them.  
**2:5** Then the Lord ·warns them [speaks to them in anger] and frightens them with his ·anger [fury].  
**2:6** He says, “I have ·appointed [installed; set] my own king over my holy mountain, Zion [the location of the Temple in Jerusalem].”  
**2:7** Now I will ·tell [recount to] you what the Lord has ·declared [decreed]: He said to me, “You are my son. Today I have become your father.  
**2:8** ·If you ask me [Ask of me], I will give you the nations as your inheritance; all the ·people on [ends of the] earth will be ·yours [your possession].  
**2:9** You will rule over them with an iron ·rod [scepter; a symbol of royal authority]. You will ·break [dash] them into pieces like pottery.”  
**2:10** ·So [Now], kings, be wise; ·rulers [rulers/judges of the earth], ·learn this lesson [be warned].  
**2:11** ·Obey [Serve] the Lord with great fear. ·Be happy [Rejoice], ·but tremble [with trembling].  
**2:12** ·Show that you are loyal to his [Kiss the] son, or ·you will be destroyed by his anger [he will be angry and you will perish on the way], because he can quickly become angry. But ·happy [blessed] are those who ·trust him for protection [find refuge in him].  

## 3장

**3:1** A Morning Prayer David sang this when he ran away from his son Absalom. Lord, ·I have many enemies [how many are my foes?]! ·Many people [How many…?] have ·turned [risen] against me.  
**3:2** Many are saying about me, “·God won’t rescue him [There is no salvation for him in God].” ·Selah [Interlude]  
**3:3** But, Lord, you are my shield [protector], my ·wonderful God [glory] who ·gives me courage [lifts up my head].  
**3:4** I will ·pray [lift my voice] to the Lord, and he will answer me from his holy mountain [Zion, the location of the Temple]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**3:5** I can lie down and go to sleep, and I will wake up again, because the Lord ·gives me strength [sustains/upholds me].  
**3:6** Thousands of troops may ·surround me [set themselves around me], but I am not afraid.  
**3:7** Lord, rise up! My God, come ·save [rescue; deliver] me! You have struck my enemies on the cheek; you have broken the teeth of the wicked.  
**3:8** ·The Lord can save his people [Salvation/Rescue/Deliverance belongs to the Lord]. ·Bless your people [May your blessing be on your people]. ·Selah [Interlude]  

## 4장

**4:1** An Evening Prayer For the director of music. With stringed instruments. A psalm of David. Answer me when I ·pray [call] to you, my God who ·does what is right [is righteous; or who vindicates me]. ·Make things easier for me [Give me room; Widen my way] when I am in trouble. Have mercy on me and hear my prayer.  
**4:2** People, how long will you turn my ·honor [glory] into shame? How long will you love what is ·false [empty] and ·look for [seek] lies [possibly referring to false gods]? ·Selah [Interlude]  
**4:3** You know that the Lord has ·chosen [set apart; distinguished] for himself those who are loyal to him [in covenant relationship with him]. The Lord listens when I ·pray [call] to him.  
**4:4** When you ·are angry [are disturbed; tremble], do not sin. ·Think about these things [Meditate; Speak to your heart] quietly ·as you go to bed [on your bed]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**4:5** ·Do what is right as a sacrifice to the Lord [Sacrifice right/righteous sacrifices] and trust the Lord.  
**4:6** Many people ask, “Who will ·give us [make us see] anything good?” Lord, ·be kind to us [let the light of your face shine on us].  
**4:7** But you have ·made me very happy [given joy to my heart], happier than they are, even with all their grain and new wine.  
**4:8** I ·go to bed [lie down] and sleep in peace, because, Lord, only you ·keep me safe [make me secure].  

## 5장

**5:1** A Morning Prayer for Protection For the director of music. For flutes. A psalm of David. Lord, ·listen [give ear] to my words. Understand my ·sadness [moans; sighs].  
**5:2** ·Listen [Pay attention] to my cry for help, my King and my God, because I pray to you.  
**5:3** Lord, every morning you hear my voice. Every morning, I ·tell you what I need [or prepare a sacrifice for you; stretch out/arrange before you], and I ·wait for your answer [watch].  
**5:4** You are not a God who ·is pleased with the wicked [takes delight in evil]; ·you do not live with those who do evil [evil does not sojourn with you].  
**5:5** Those people who ·make fun of you [or boast] cannot stand before ·you [your eyes]. You hate all those who do evil.  
**5:6** You destroy ·liars [those who speak lies]; the Lord ·hates [despises] those ·who kill and trick others [with bloodguilt and deceit].  
**5:7** Because of your great ·love [loyalty; covenant love], I ·can [or will] come into your ·Temple [house]. Because I ·fear you [hold you in awe], I can ·worship [bow down] ·in [or toward] your holy Temple.  
**5:8** Lord, since I have many enemies, ·show me the right thing to do [lead/guide me in your righteousness]. ·Show me clearly how you want me to live [Make your way straight before me].  
**5:9** My enemies’ mouths do not tell the truth; ·in their hearts they want to destroy others [their innards are destruction]. Their throats are like open graves ; they use their tongues for ·telling lies [flattery].  
**5:10** God, ·declare them guilty [make them bear their iniquity]! Let them fall ·into their own traps [or by their own advice]. ·Send [Cast] them away because their ·sins [transgressions] are many; they have ·turned [rebelled] against you.  
**5:11** But let everyone who ·trusts [finds refuge in] you ·be happy [rejoice]; let them sing glad songs forever. ·Protect [Spread your protection on] those who love you and ·who are happy because of you [let those who love your name rejoice in you].  
**5:12** Lord, you bless those who ·do what is right [are righteous]; you ·protect them [surround them with favor] like a shield.  

## 6장

**6:1** A Prayer for Mercy in Troubled Times For the director of music. With stringed instruments. Upon the ·sheminith [eighth; a reference to an eight-stringed instrument or possibly the manner of singing]. A psalm of David. Lord, don’t ·correct [rebuke; reprove] me when you are angry; don’t ·punish [discipline] me when you are ·very angry [enraged].  
**6:2** Lord, ·have mercy on [be gracious to] me because I ·am weak [languish; faint]. Heal me, Lord, because my bones ·ache [are in agony].  
**6:3** I ·am very upset [ache; am in agony]. Lord, how long will it be?  
**6:4** Lord, return and save me; ·save [rescue; deliver] me because of your ·kindness [loyalty; covenant love].  
**6:5** Dead people don’t remember you; those in ·the grave [or the underworld; Sheol] don’t praise you.  
**6:6** I am ·tired [weary] ·of crying to you [because of my moaning]. Every night ·my bed is wet [I flood my pillow] with tears; my bed is soaked from my crying.  
**6:7** My eyes are weak ·from so much crying [because of my grief]; they are weak ·from crying about [because of] my enemies.  
**6:8** Get away from me, all you who do evil, because the Lord has heard my ·crying [supplication].  
**6:9** The Lord has heard my cry for help; the Lord will ·answer [accept] my prayer.  
**6:10** All my enemies will be ashamed and ·troubled [in agony]. They will turn and suddenly leave in shame.  

## 7장

**7:1** A Prayer for Fairness A shiggaion [a musical or literary term of uncertain meaning] of David which he sang to the Lord about Cush, from the tribe of Benjamin [an unknown person, but the tribe of Benjamin, Saul’s tribe, resisted David’s kingship at first]. Lord my God, I ·trust in you for protection [find refuge in you]. ·Save [Rescue; Deliver] me and rescue me from those who are ·chasing [pursuing] me.  
**7:2** Otherwise, like a lion they will tear me apart. They will ·rip me to pieces [or drag me away], and no one can ·save [rescue; deliver] me.  
**7:3** Lord my God, what have I done? Have my hands done something ·wrong [to make me guilty]?  
**7:4** Have I done wrong to ·my friend [the one at peace with me] or stolen without reason from my enemy?  
**7:5** If I have, let my enemy ·chase [pursue] me and ·capture [overtake] me. Let him trample ·me [my life] into the ·dust [earth] and ·bury me [lay my honor] in the ground. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**7:6** Lord, rise up in your anger; ·stand up [lift yourself up] against my enemies’ ·anger [fury]. ·Get up [Wake up], ·my God [or for me], and ·demand fairness [insist on/command judgment].  
**7:7** Gather the ·nations [assembly/congregations of the peoples] around you and ·rule [or take a seat over; return] them from ·above [on high].  
**7:8** Lord, judge the people. Lord, ·defend [judge] me ·because I am right [according to my righteousness], ·because I have done no wrong [according to my innocence].  
**7:9** God, you ·do what is right [are righteous]. You ·know [test] our ·thoughts [hearts/minds] and ·feelings [kidneys; the seat of emotions in Hebrew thought]. Stop those wicked actions done by evil people, and ·help [establish] those who ·do what is right [are righteous].  
**7:10** God ·protects me like a [is my] shield; he saves those whose hearts ·are right [have integrity].  
**7:11** God ·judges by what is right [is a righteous judge], and God is ·always ready to punish the wicked [angry every day].  
**7:12** If they do not ·change their lives [repent], God will sharpen his sword; he will string his bow and take aim.  
**7:13** He has prepared his deadly weapons; he has made his flaming arrows.  
**7:14** There are people who ·think up [conceive] evil and ·plan [are pregnant with] ·trouble [malice] and ·tell [give birth to] lies.  
**7:15** They dig a ·hole [pit] ·to trap others [and dig it deep], but they will fall into it themselves.  
**7:16** ·They will get themselves into trouble [Their trouble/malice will return to their head]; the violence they cause will ·hurt only themselves [come down on their heads].  
**7:17** I praise the Lord ·because he does what is right [according to his righteousness]. I sing praises to the Lord Most High.  

## 8장

**8:1** The Lord’s Greatness For the director of music. On the gittith [perhaps a musical term or instrument]. A psalm of David. Lord our Lord, ·Your name is the most wonderful name [How majestic is your name] in all the earth ! ·It brings you praise [You have set your splendor/glory] in heaven above.  
**8:2** ·You have taught children and babies to sing praises to you [Out of the mouth of babies and infants you have established/founded strength] because of your enemies. And so you silence your enemies and those who try to get ·even [revenge].  
**8:3** I look at your heavens, ·which you made with [the work of] your fingers. I see the moon and stars, which you ·created [established].  
**8:4** ·But why are people even important to you [What are people that you remember them]? Why do you take care of ·human beings [the son of man]?  
**8:5** You made them a little lower than ·the angels [or God] and crowned them with glory and honor.  
**8:6** You ·put them in charge of [give them rule over] ·everything you made [the work of your hands]. You put all things under their ·control [feet]:  
**8:7** all the sheep, the cattle, and the ·wild animals [beasts of the field],  
**8:8** the birds in the ·sky [heavens], the fish in the sea, and everything that ·lives under water [passes/travels on the paths of the sea].  
**8:9** Lord our Lord, ·your name is the most wonderful [how majestic is your] name in all the earth!  

## 9장

**9:1** Thanksgiving for Victory For the director of music. To the tune of “The Death of the Son.” A psalm of David. I will ·praise [or give thanks to] you, Lord, with all my heart. I will ·tell [recount] all ·the miracles you have done [your wonderful deeds].  
**9:2** I will be happy and rejoice because of you; God Most High, I will sing praises to your name.  
**9:3** My enemies turn back; they ·are overwhelmed [stumble] and ·die [perish] ·because of [or before] you.  
**9:4** You have ·heard [upheld; maintained] my ·complaint [just cause; or right and my cause]; you sat on your throne and judged ·by what was right [righteously].  
**9:5** You ·spoke strongly against the [rebuked; reproved] foreign nations and destroyed the wicked; you ·wiped out [blotted out; erased] their names forever and ever.  
**9:6** The enemy is ·gone [done; a ruin] forever. You ·destroyed [uprooted] their cities; ·no one even remembers them [their memory perishes].  
**9:7** But the Lord ·rules [is enthroned; sits] forever. He ·sits on his throne to judge [has established his throne for justice/judgment],  
**9:8** and he will judge the world ·in fairness [with righteousness]; he will decide what is fair for the ·nations [peoples].  
**9:9** The Lord ·defends [is a refuge for] those who ·suffer [are oppressed/exploited]; ·he defends them [a refuge] in times of ·trouble [distress].  
**9:10** Those who know ·the Lord [your name] trust ·him [you], because ·he [you] will not ·leave [abandon; forsake] those who ·come to him [seek you].  
**9:11** Sing praises to the Lord who ·is king on Mount [dwells on] Zion [the location of the Temple]. Tell ·the nations [among the peoples] what he has done.  
**9:12** ·He remembers who the murderers are [or The one who avenges blood remembers them; God]; he will not forget the cries of those who suffer.  
**9:13** Lord, ·have mercy on [be gracious toward] me. See how ·my enemies [those who hate me] ·hurt [afflict; persecute] me. ·Do not let me go through [Lift me up from] the gates of death.  
**9:14** Then, at the gates of ·Jerusalem [the daughter of Zion; a name of Jerusalem], I will ·praise you [declare your praises]; I will rejoice ·because you saved me [in your salvation].  
**9:15** The nations have ·fallen [sunk] into the pit they ·dug [made]. Their feet are caught in the nets they ·laid [hid].  
**9:16** The Lord has made himself known by ·his fair decisions [the judgments he has made]; the wicked get trapped by ·what they do [the deeds of their palms/hands]. ·Higgaion [a musical notation]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**9:17** Wicked people will ·go [return] to the ·grave [or underworld; Sheol], and so will all ·those who [the nations that] forget God.  
**9:18** But those who ·have troubles [are afflicted] will not always be forgotten. The hopes of the ·poor [oppressed; exploited] will never ·die [perish].  
**9:19** Lord, rise up and don’t let people ·think they are strong [prevail]. Judge the nations in your presence.  
**9:20** ·Teach them to fear you [or Strike them with terror], Lord. The nations must learn that they are ·only human [mere mortals]. ·Selah [Interlude]  

## 10장

**10:1** A Complaint About Evil People Lord, why ·are you [do you stand] so far away? Why do you hide ·when there is [in times of] ·trouble [distress]?  
**10:2** Proudly the wicked ·chase down [hunt down; persecute] ·those who suffer [or the poor]. Let them be caught in ·their own traps [the schemes they have thought up].  
**10:3** They ·brag [boast] about the ·things they want [cravings/desires of their soul]. ·They bless the greedy but [or The greedy curse and] ·hate [reject] the Lord.  
**10:4** The wicked people are too proud. They do not ·look for [pursue; seek] God; there is no room for God in their thoughts.  
**10:5** ·They always succeed [Their ways are always successful/prosperous]. ·They are far from keeping your laws [Your judgments are above their grasp]; they ·make fun of [sneer/scoff at] their enemies.  
**10:6** They say ·to themselves [in their hearts], “·Nothing bad will ever happen to me [I will never be moved/shaken]; I will never ·be ruined [have trouble/be harmed].”  
**10:7** Their mouths are full of curses, ·lies [deceit], and ·threats [violence]; ·they use their tongues for [under their tongues is] ·sin [trouble] and evil.  
**10:8** They ·hide [lie in ambush] near the villages. They ·look for innocent people to kill [murder the innocent]; ·they watch in secret [their eyes look intently] for the helpless.  
**10:9** They ·wait in hiding [lie in ambush in a covert/cover] like a lion. They ·wait [lie] to catch poor people; they catch the poor in nets and drag them off.  
**10:10** The poor are crushed and thrown down; they ·are defeated [fall] ·because the others are stronger [by their might].  
**10:11** The wicked ·think [say in their hearts], “God has forgotten us. He ·doesn’t see what is happening [has hidden his face and does not see anything].”  
**10:12** Lord, rise up and ·punish the wicked [raise your hand, God]. Don’t forget those who ·need help [are oppressed].  
**10:13** Why do wicked people ·hate [despise] God? They say ·to themselves [in their hearts], “·God won’t punish us [You will not pursue].”  
**10:14** Lord, surely you see these ·cruel [troublesome] and ·evil [grievous] things; look at them and ·do something [put it in your hands]. ·People in trouble [The helpless] ·look to you for help [entrust/abandon themselves to your hands]. You are the one who helps the orphans.  
**10:15** Break the ·power [hand] of wicked and evil people. ·Punish them for the evil they have done [You will seek out their wickedness until you find none].  
**10:16** The Lord is King forever and ever. ·Destroy from your land those nations [The nations will perish from his land].  
**10:17** Lord, you have heard ·what the poor people want [the desires of the poor]. ·Do what they ask [You will strengthen their heart], and ·listen to them [you will cause your ear to pay attention].  
**10:18** ·Protect [Bring justice to] the orphans and ·put an end to suffering [the oppressed] so ·they will no longer be afraid of evil people [those from the earth may terrify no more].  

## 11장

**11:1** Trust in the Lord For the director of music. Of David. I ·trust in the Lord for protection [find refuge in the Lord]. So why do you say to me, “·Fly [Flee] like a bird to your mountain.  
**11:2** For, look, the wicked ·string [bend] their bows; they set their arrows on the bowstrings. They shoot from dark places at those who are ·honest [upright/virtuous in heart].  
**11:3** When the foundations [a stable society] collapse, what can ·good [righteous] people do?”  
**11:4** The Lord is in his holy Temple; the Lord sits on his throne in heaven. ·He sees what people do [His eyes watch]; ·he keeps his eye on them [his gaze examines/tests people].  
**11:5** The Lord ·tests [examines] ·those who do right and those who do wrong [the righteous and the wicked], but he hates those who love ·to hurt others [violence].  
**11:6** He will ·send [rain] hot coals and burning sulfur on the wicked. A ·whirlwind [scorching wind] is ·what they will get [the portion of their cup; an image of judgment].  
**11:7** The Lord ·does what is right [is righteous], and he loves ·justice [righteousness], so ·honest people [the upright/virtuous] will see his face.  

## 12장

**12:1** A Prayer Against Liars For the director of music. Upon the ·sheminith [eighth; a reference to an eight-stringed instrument or possibly the manner of singing]. A psalm of David. Save me, Lord, because the ·good [faithful; godly; covenantal; loyal] people are all gone; ·no true believers are left on earth [the faithful have vanished among humanity].  
**12:2** Everyone ·lies [speaks falsehood] to his neighbors; they ·say one thing and mean another [speak with flattering lips and with a double heart/heart and heart].  
**12:3** The Lord will ·stop [cut off] those flattering lips and those bragging tongues.  
**12:4** They say, “Our tongues will ·help us win [prevail]. ·We can say what we wish [Our lips belong to us]; ·no one [who…?] is our master.”  
**12:5** But the Lord says, “I will now rise up, because the ·poor [weak] are ·being hurt [destroyed; plundered; oppressed]. Because of the ·moans [groans; sighs] of the ·helpless [needy], I will give them the ·help [victory] they ·want [long for].”  
**12:6** The Lord’s ·words [or promises] are ·pure [flawless], like silver ·purified [refined] ·by fire [or in a furnace], ·purified [refined] seven times over.  
**12:7** Lord, you will ·keep us safe [guard/protect them]; you will always ·protect [guard] us from such ·people [a generation].  
**12:8** But the wicked ·are [walk] all around us; ·everyone loves what is wrong [what is vile is lifted up among the sons of man/humanity].  

## 13장

**13:1** A Prayer for God to Be Near For the director of music. A psalm of David. How long will you forget me, Lord? Forever? How long will you hide your face from me?  
**13:2** How long must I ·worry [or bear pain; hold counsels] and ·feel sad [hold sorrow] in my heart all day? How long will my enemy ·win [rise up] over me?  
**13:3** Lord, look at me. Answer me, my God; ·tell me [light up my eyes], or I will ·die [sleep the sleep of death].  
**13:4** Otherwise my enemy will say, “I have ·won [finished him off]!” ·Those against me [My foes] will rejoice that I’ve been ·defeated [shaken; moved].  
**13:5** I ·trust [have confidence] in your ·love [loyalty; covenant love]. My heart ·is happy [rejoices] because ·you saved me [of your victory/salvation].  
**13:6** I sing to the Lord because he has ·taken care of [been good to] me.  

## 14장

**14:1** The Unbelieving Fool For the director of music. Of David. Fools say ·to themselves [in their hearts], “There is no God [largely parallels this psalm].” ·Fools are evil [They are corrupt] and do ·terrible [detestable] things ; there is no one who does anything good.  
**14:2** The Lord looked down from heaven on all people to see if anyone ·understood [was wise/insightful], if anyone was ·looking to God for help [seeking God].  
**14:3** But all have ·turned [wandered] away. Together, everyone has become ·evil [perverse]. There is no one who does anything good, not even one.  
**14:4** Don’t ·the wicked [those who do evil] ·understand [know]? They ·destroy [consume; eat] my people as if they were ·eating [consuming] bread. They do not ·ask the Lord for help [call on the Lord].  
**14:5** But the wicked are ·filled [terrified] with terror, because God is with ·those who do what is right [the company of the righteous].  
**14:6** The wicked ·upset [confuse; frustrate] the plans of the poor, but the Lord ·will protect them [is their refuge].  
**14:7** I pray that ·victory [salvation] will come to Israel from Mount Zion [the location of the Temple]! May the Lord ·bring them back [restore the fortunes of his people; perhaps at the end of the exile]. Then the people of Jacob will rejoice, and the people of Israel will be glad.  

## 15장

**15:1** What the Lord Demands A psalm of David. Lord, who may ·enter [dwell/abide/sojourn in] your Holy Tent [the Tabernacle]? Who may live on your holy mountain [Mount Zion]?  
**15:2** Only those who ·are innocent [walk innocently] and who do ·what is right [righteousness]. Such people speak the truth from their hearts  
**15:3** and do not ·tell lies about others [slander with their tongue]. They do no ·wrong [evil] to their neighbors and do not ·gossip [raise a reproachful matter with their associates].  
**15:4** ·They do not respect hateful people [The wicked are despised in their eyes] but honor those who ·honor [fear] the Lord. They keep their promises to their neighbors, even when it hurts.  
**15:5** They do not charge interest on money they lend and do not take ·money [a bribe] to hurt innocent people. Whoever does all these things will never be ·destroyed [moved].  

## 16장

**16:1** The Lord Takes Care of His People A miktam [perhaps “inscription”] of David. ·Protect [Guard] me, God, because I ·trust [take refuge] in you.  
**16:2** I said to the Lord, “You are my Lord. ·Every good thing I have comes from you [I have no good apart from you].”  
**16:3** As for the ·godly people [holy ones; saints] in the ·world [or land], they are the ·wonderful [noble] ones I ·enjoy [take pleasure in].  
**16:4** But those who ·turn to [run/hurry after] ·idols [other gods] ·will have much [multiply] pain. I will not ·offer [pour out offerings of] blood to those idols or even ·speak [take on my lips] their names.  
**16:5** No, the Lord is ·all I need [my portion and my cup]. ·He takes care of me [You hold my lot; a device like the Urim and Thummim whereby God reveals one’s future].  
**16:6** ·My share in life has been pleasant [The boundary lines fall for me in pleasant places]; my ·part [inheritance] has been beautiful.  
**16:7** I ·praise [bless] the Lord because he advises me. Even at night, ·I feel his leading [my innards instruct me].  
**16:8** I keep the Lord before me always. Because he is ·close by my side [at my right hand], I will not be ·hurt [moved].  
**16:9** So ·I rejoice and am glad [my heart exults and my glory/soul/or innards is glad]. Even my body ·has hope [dwells securely],  
**16:10** because you will not ·leave [abandon] me in ·the grave [or the underworld; Sheol]. You will not let your ·holy one [saint; loyal one] ·rot [see the Pit; the grave].  
**16:11** You will teach me ·how to live a holy [the path of] life. ·Being with you will fill me with joy [In your face/presence is the fullness of joy]; at your right hand I will find pleasure forever.  

## 17장

**17:1** A Prayer for Protection A prayer of David. Lord, hear ·me begging for fairness [a just cause]; ·listen [pay attention] to my cry for help. ·Pay attention [Bend your ear] to my prayer, because ·I speak the truth [my lips are not deceitful].  
**17:2** ·You will judge that I am right [My judgment/vindication will come from before you]; your eyes can see what is ·true [virtuous].  
**17:3** You have examined my heart; you have ·tested me all [or visited me at] night. You ·questioned [tested] me without finding anything wrong; ·I have not sinned with my mouth [my mouth has not transgressed].  
**17:4** ·I have obeyed your commands [As for the deeds of people, by the word of your lips], ·so I have not done what evil people do [I have kept away from the ways of the violent].  
**17:5** ·I have done what you told me [My steps have held fast to your paths]; ·I have not failed [My feet have not slipped].  
**17:6** I call to you, ·and [for] you answer me, O God. ·Listen [Extend your ear] to me now, and hear what I say.  
**17:7** Your ·love [loyalty; covenant love] is wonderful. ·By your power [At your right hand] you save those who ·trust [find their refuge in] you from ·their enemies [those who rise up against them].  
**17:8** Protect me as ·you would protect your own [or the apple/pupil of your] eye. Hide me under the shadow of your wings.  
**17:9** Keep me from the wicked who ·attack [mistreat] me, from my enemies who surround me.  
**17:10** They ·are selfish [close their callous hearts] and ·brag about themselves [their mouths speak proudly].  
**17:11** They have ·chased [tracked] me until they have surrounded me. They ·plan [set their eyes] to throw me to the ground.  
**17:12** They are like lions ready to ·kill [tear up prey]; like lions, they sit in ·hiding [ambush; cover].  
**17:13** Lord, rise up, ·face [confront] the enemy, and ·throw them down [subdue them]. ·Save [Rescue; Deliver] me from the wicked with your sword.  
**17:14** Lord, save me by your ·power [hand] from mortals, from mortals whose ·reward [portion] in the world is in this life. ·They have plenty of food [Your stores have filled their bellies]. ·They have many sons [or Their sons have plenty] and leave ·much money [their surplus] to their children.  
**17:15** ·Because I have lived right [In righteousness], I will see your face. When I wake up, I will see your likeness and be satisfied.  

## 18장

**18:1** A Song of Victory For the director of music. By the Lord’s servant, David. David sang this song to the Lord ·when [on the day] the Lord had ·saved [rescued; delivered] him from Saul and all his other enemies [the occasion is unknown; parallels this psalm]. He said: I love you, Lord. You are my strength.  
**18:2** The Lord is my rock, my ·protection [fortress], my ·Savior [rescuer; deliverer]. My God is my rock. ·I can run to him for safety […in whom I find protection/take refuge]. He is my shield and ·my saving strength [the horn of my salvation; symbolizing strength based on an animal lifting its head triumphantly], my ·defender [stronghold].  
**18:3** I ·will call to [call upon] the Lord, who is worthy of praise, and I ·will be [or am] saved from my enemies.  
**18:4** The ·ropes [cords] of death ·came around [swirled about; encompassed] me; the ·deadly rivers [floods/torrents of destruction] overwhelmed me.  
**18:5** The ·ropes [cords] of death ·wrapped around [entangled; coiled around] me. The ·traps [snares] of death ·were before [confronted; lay ahead of] me.  
**18:6** In my ·trouble [distress; anguish] I ·called [cried out] to the Lord. I ·cried out [called] to my God for help. From his ·Temple [sanctuary] he heard my voice; my ·call for help [cry] reached his ears.  
**18:7** The earth ·trembled [reeled; quaked] and ·shook [rocked]. The foundations of the mountains began to ·shake [shudder]. They ·trembled [reeled; quaked] because the Lord was angry.  
**18:8** Smoke ·came out of his nose [poured/rose from his nostrils], and ·burning [devouring] fire came out of his mouth. Burning coals ·went before [blazed/flamed out from] him.  
**18:9** He ·tore open [parted] the ·sky [heavens] and came down with ·dark clouds [storm clouds; thick darkness] under his feet.  
**18:10** He rode a ·creature with wings [cherub; a mighty spiritual being/angel] and flew. ·He raced […soaring] on the wings of the wind.  
**18:11** He made darkness his covering, his ·shelter [canopy; shroud] around him, surrounded by ·fog [thick rain] and clouds.  
**18:12** Out of the brightness ·of his presence [before him] came clouds with hail and ·lightning [fiery coals].  
**18:13** The Lord thundered from heaven; the ·Most High raised his voice [voice of the Most High resounded], and there was hail and ·lightning [fiery coals].  
**18:14** He shot his arrows and scattered his enemies. His many bolts of lightning ·confused them with fear [routed them].  
**18:15** ·Lord, you spoke strongly [At your rebuke, O Lord…]. ·The wind blew from your nose […at the blast of breath from your nostrils…]. Then the ·valleys [floor; channels] of the sea ·appeared [were exposed], and the foundations of the earth were ·seen [laid bare].  
**18:16** The Lord reached down from ·above [heaven; on high] and ·took [rescued] me; he ·pulled me from the deep water [drew me out of mighty waters].  
**18:17** He ·saved [rescued; delivered] me from my powerful enemies, from those who hated me, because they were too strong for me.  
**18:18** They ·attacked [confronted] me ·at my time of trouble [in the day of my distress/calamity/disaster], but the Lord ·supported me [was my stay].  
**18:19** He took me to a ·safe [spacious; open; broad] place. Because he delights in me, he ·saved [rescued; delivered] me.  
**18:20** The Lord ·spared [rewarded] me ·because I did what was right [according to my righteousness]. Because ·I have not done evil [of my innocence; of the cleanness of my hands], he has ·rewarded [restored] me.  
**18:21** I have ·followed [obeyed; kept; guarded] the ways of the Lord; I have not done evil by turning away from my God.  
**18:22** I ·remember [follow; have before me] all his ·laws [regulations] and have not ·broken [abandoned; turned aside from] his ·rules [statutes; ordinances; requirements].  
**18:23** I am ·innocent [blameless] before him; I have kept myself from ·doing evil [sin; guilt; iniquity].  
**18:24** The Lord ·rewarded [repaid] me ·because I did what was right [according to my righteousness], ·because I did what the Lord said was right [according to my cleanness/purity in his sight].  
**18:25** Lord, you ·are [show yourself] ·loyal [faithful; kind] to those who are ·loyal [faithful; kind], and you are good to those who are good.  
**18:26** You ·are [show yourself] ·pure [sincere] to those who are ·pure [sincere], but you ·are [show yourself] ·against [hostile/shrewd/cunning/perverse to] those who are ·bad [perverse; devious; crooked].  
**18:27** You ·save [rescue; deliver] the ·humble [afflicted], but you ·bring down [watch and humiliate] ·those who are proud [the haughty].  
**18:28** Lord, you give light to my lamp. My God ·brightens the darkness around me [lights up/illuminates my darkness].  
**18:29** With your help I can ·attack [crush] an army. With God’s help I can ·jump over [scale] a wall.  
**18:30** The ·ways [way; path] of God are ·without fault [blameless; perfect]. The Lord’s ·words [promises] are ·pure [tested; flawless; proven true]. He is a shield to those who ·trust [seek protection/take refuge in] him.  
**18:31** Who is God? Only the Lord. Who is the Rock? Only our God.  
**18:32** God ·is my protection [is my strong fortress; or girds me with strength]. He makes my way ·free from fault [perfect; secure; a wide path].  
**18:33** He makes ·me [my feet] like a deer that does not stumble [sure-footed]; he ·helps me stand [sets me] on the ·steep mountains [heights].  
**18:34** He trains my hands for battle so my arms can bend a bronze bow.  
**18:35** You ·protect me with your saving shield [have given me the shield of your salvation/victory]. You support me with your right hand. ·You have stooped to make [Your help makes] me great.  
**18:36** You ·give me a better way to live [broaden my path; widen my steps beneath me], so ·I live as you want me to [my feet do not slip; my ankles do not weaken].  
**18:37** I ·chased [pursued] my enemies and ·caught [exterminated] them. I did not ·quit [turn back] until they were ·destroyed [annihilated; consumed].  
**18:38** I ·crushed them [shattered them; struck them down] so they couldn’t ·rise [get] up again. They fell beneath my feet.  
**18:39** You ·gave me [armed/girded me with] strength ·in [for] battle. You ·made my enemies bow [humbled/subdued my enemies] ·before me [or under my feet].  
**18:40** You made my enemies ·turn back [turn their backs; retreat], and I destroyed ·those who hated me [my foes].  
**18:41** They ·called for help [looked around], but no one came to ·save [rescue; deliver] them. They ·called [looked] to the Lord, but he did not answer them.  
**18:42** I ·beat my enemies into pieces [ground/pulverized them], like dust in the wind. I poured them out like ·mud [mire] in the streets.  
**18:43** You ·saved [rescued; delivered] me when the people ·attacked me [quarreled; fought]. You made me the ·leader [ruler; head] of nations. People I never knew serve me.  
**18:44** As soon as they hear ·me [or of me], they obey me. Foreigners ·obey [cower/cringe before] me.  
**18:45** They all ·become afraid [lose heart/their courage] and ·tremble in [come trembling from] their ·hiding places [fortresses; strongholds].  
**18:46** The Lord lives! May my Rock be ·praised [blessed]. Praise the God ·who saves me […of my salvation]!  
**18:47** God gives me ·victory [revenge; vengeance] over my enemies and brings ·people [nations] under ·my rule [me].  
**18:48** He ·saves [rescues; delivers] me from my enemies. You ·set me over those who hate me [exalt/lift me above my enemies]. You ·saved [rescued; delivered] me from violent people.  
**18:49** So I will ·praise [extol] you, Lord, among the nations. I will sing praises to your name.  
**18:50** The Lord ·gives great victories [is a tower of salvation] to his king. He ·is loyal [shows kindness/faithful love] to his ·appointed king [anointed], to David and his descendants forever.  

## 19장

**19:1** God’s Works and Word For the director of music. A psalm of David. The heavens ·declare [recount; narrate] the glory [the manifest presence] of God, and the ·skies [firmament; dome] ·announce [speak out] ·what his hands have made [the works of his hands].  
**19:2** Day after day they ·tell the story [bubble/pour forth speech]; night after night they ·tell it again [declare knowledge].  
**19:3** They have no speech or words; they have no voice to be heard.  
**19:4** But their ·message [line] goes out through all the world; their words go ·everywhere on [to the ends of the] earth. ·The sky [Heaven; In them] is like a ·home [tent] for the sun.  
**19:5** The sun comes out like a bridegroom from his ·bedroom [wedding chamber]. It rejoices like an ·athlete [strong man] eager to run a race.  
**19:6** The sun rises at one end of the ·sky [heavens] and ·follows its path [its circuit is] to the other end. Nothing hides from its heat.  
**19:7** The ·teachings [laws; instructions] of the Lord are perfect; they ·give new strength [revive the soul]. The ·rules [testimonies] of the Lord ·can be trusted [are faithful/sure]; they make ·plain [simpleminded; immature] people wise.  
**19:8** The orders of the Lord are ·right [virtuous]; ·they make people happy [rejoicing the heart]. The commands of the Lord are pure; ·they light up the way [giving light to the eyes].  
**19:9** ·Respect [Fear] for the Lord is ·good [pure]; it will ·last [endure] forever. The judgments of the Lord are true; they are ·completely [altogether] ·right [righteous].  
**19:10** They are ·worth [desired; coveted] more than gold, even ·the purest [fine] gold. They are sweeter than honey, even the ·finest honey [droppings of the honeycomb].  
**19:11** By them your servant is warned. ·Keeping [Observing] them brings great reward.  
**19:12** ·People cannot [Who can…?] ·see [detect; understand] their own ·mistakes [errors]. ·Forgive [Clear] me for my ·secret [hidden] sins.  
**19:13** ·Keep me [Restrain your servant] from ·the sins of pride [proud thoughts]; don’t let them ·rule [dominate] me. Then I can be pure and innocent of the greatest of sins.  
**19:14** ·I hope my ·words [The words of my mouth] and ·thoughts [meditations of my heart] ·please [be before] you. Lord, you are my Rock, ·the one who saves me [and my redeemer].  

## 20장

**20:1** A Prayer for the King For the director of music. A psalm of David. May the Lord answer you in ·times [the day] of ·trouble [distress]. May the name of the God of Jacob ·protect [defend; provide refuge for] you.  
**20:2** May he send you help from ·his Temple [the sanctuary] and support you from Mount Zion [location of the Temple].  
**20:3** May he remember all your ·offerings [gifts; tributes; grain offerings] and ·accept [look with favor on] all your ·sacrifices [whole burnt offerings]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**20:4** May he give you ·what you want [all your heart] and ·make all your plans succeed [fulfill all your plans],  
**20:5** and we will shout for joy when you ·succeed [are victorious; as in battle], and we will raise a ·flag [banner] in the name of our God. May the Lord ·give you [fulfill] all that you ask for.  
**20:6** Now I know the Lord ·helps [saves; gives victory to] his ·appointed king [anointed]. He answers him from his holy heaven and ·saves him [gives him victory] with his strong right hand.  
**20:7** Some ·trust in [boast in; rely on] chariots, others in horses, but we ·trust [boast in; rely on] the name of the Lord our God.  
**20:8** They ·are overwhelmed and defeated [collapse and fall], but we ·march forward and win [rise and stand erect].  
**20:9** Lord, ·save [give victory to] the king! Answer us when we call for help.  

## 21장

**21:1** Thanksgiving for the King For the director of music. A psalm of David. Lord, the king rejoices because of your strength; he is so happy when you ·save him [give him victory/help]!  
**21:2** You gave the king ·what he wanted [the desire of his heart] and did not ·refuse [withhold] ·what he asked for [the request of his lips]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**21:3** You put ·good things [rich blessings] before him and placed a gold crown on his head.  
**21:4** He asked you for life, and you gave it to him, ·so his years go on and on [length of days forever and ever].  
**21:5** He has great glory because you gave him ·victories [help]; you gave him ·honor [splendor] and ·praise [majesty].  
**21:6** You ·always [forever] gave him blessings; you made him glad because ·you were with him [of the joy of your presence].  
**21:7** The king truly ·trusts [has confidence in] the Lord. Because God Most High always ·loves [is loyal toward] him, he will not be ·overwhelmed [moved].  
**21:8** Your hand ·is against [will find out] all your enemies; ·those who hate you will feel your power [your right hand will find out those who hate you].  
**21:9** When ·you [the Lord] appear, you will burn them as in a furnace. In your anger you will swallow them up, and fire will burn them up.  
**21:10** You will destroy their ·families [offspring] from the earth; their ·children [seed] will not live.  
**21:11** They made evil plans against you, but ·their traps [the schemes they devise] won’t ·work [succeed].  
**21:12** You will make them turn their backs when you aim your arrows at ·them [their faces].  
**21:13** Be ·supreme [exalted], Lord, in your power. We sing and praise your ·greatness [strength].  

## 22장

**22:1** The Prayer of a Suffering Man For the director of music. To the tune of “The Doe of Dawn.” A psalm of David. My God, my God, why have you ·abandoned [forsaken] me ? You seem far from ·saving [helping] me, far away from my groans.  
**22:2** My God, I call to you during the day, but you do not answer. I call at night; I ·am not silent [or get no rest].  
**22:3** You ·sit as the Holy One [are holy]. ·The praises of Israel are your throne [You are enthroned on the praises of Israel].  
**22:4** Our ·ancestors [fathers] ·trusted [had confidence in] you; they ·trusted [had confidence], and you ·saved [rescued; delivered] them.  
**22:5** They ·called [cried out] to you for help and were rescued. They ·trusted [had confidence in] you and were not ·disappointed [shamed; humiliated].  
**22:6** But I am like a worm instead of ·a man [human]. People ·make fun of [scorn; reproach] me and ·hate [despise] me.  
**22:7** Those who look at me ·laugh [ridicule/mock me]. They ·stick out their tongues [throw insults; open lips] and shake their heads.  
**22:8** They say, “·Turn to the Lord for help [Trust the Lord]. Maybe he will ·save [rescue; deliver] you. If he ·likes [delights/takes pleasure in] you, maybe he will ·rescue [save; deliver] you.”  
**22:9** You ·had my mother give birth to me [brought me out of the womb]. You made me ·trust [have confidence in] you while I was ·just a baby [at the breasts of my mother].  
**22:10** ·I have leaned on you since the day I was born [On you I was cast from the womb]; you have been ·my God [mine] ·since my mother gave me birth [from the womb of my mother].  
**22:11** So don’t be far away from me. Now ·trouble [distress] is near, and there is no one to help.  
**22:12** People have surrounded me like ·angry [many] bulls. Like the strong bulls of Bashan, they ·are on every side [encircle me].  
**22:13** Like ·hungry [rending; tearing their prey], roaring lions they open their mouths at me.  
**22:14** My strength is gone, like water poured out onto the ground, and my bones are out of joint. My heart is like wax [weak, formless]; it has melted inside me.  
**22:15** My strength has dried up like a ·clay pot [potsherd], and my tongue sticks to the top of my mouth. You laid me in the dust of death.  
**22:16** Evil people have surrounded me; like dogs ·they [a group of evil people] have trapped me. They have ·bitten [pierced; or shriveled] my ·arms [hands] and ·legs [feet].  
**22:17** I can count all my bones; people look and stare at me.  
**22:18** They divided my clothes among them, and they ·threw [cast] lots for my clothing.  
**22:19** But, Lord, don’t be far away. You are my ·strength [or help]; hurry to help me.  
**22:20** ·Save [Rescue; Deliver] me from the sword; save my life from the dogs.  
**22:21** ·Rescue [Save; Deliver] me from the lion’s mouth; ·save [answer] me from the horns of the bulls.  
**22:22** Then I will ·tell my brothers and sisters about you [recount your name/reputation to my brothers]; I will praise you in the ·public meeting [assembly; congregation].  
**22:23** Praise the Lord, all you who ·respect [fear] him. All you ·descendants [seed] of Jacob [Israelites], ·honor [glorify] him; ·fear [revere] him, all you Israelites.  
**22:24** He does not ·ignore [despise or disdain] ·those in trouble [the suffering of the afflicted]. He doesn’t hide his face from them but listens when they ·call out to him [cry to him for help].  
**22:25** Lord, ·I praise you [from you comes my praise] in the great ·meeting of your people [assembly]; these ·worshipers [who fear him] will see me ·do what I promised [fulfill my vows].  
**22:26** ·Poor [or Afflicted] people will eat ·until they are full [and be satisfied]; those who ·look to the Lord [seek him] will praise him. May your hearts live forever!  
**22:27** ·People everywhere [All the ends of the earth] will remember and will turn to the Lord. All the families of the nations will worship him  
**22:28** because ·the Lord is King [rule belongs to the Lord], and he rules the nations.  
**22:29** All the ·powerful people [fat ones] on earth will eat and worship. Everyone will ·bow down to [kneel before] him, all who will ·one day die [go down to the dust and cannot keep themselves alive].  
**22:30** The ·people in the future [posterity; seed] will serve him; they will always be told about the Lord.  
**22:31** They will ·tell that he does what is right [recount his righteousness]. People who are not yet born will hear what God has done.  

## 23장

**23:1** The Lord the Shepherd A psalm of David. The Lord is my shepherd; I ·have everything I need [will lack nothing].  
**23:2** He ·lets me rest [makes me lie down] in green pastures. He leads me to ·calm [quiet] water.  
**23:3** He ·gives me new strength [renews my soul]. He leads me on paths that are ·right [righteous; or straight] for the ·good [sake] of his ·name [reputation].  
**23:4** Even if I walk through ·a very dark valley [or the shadow of death], I will ·not be afraid [fear no evil], because you are with me. Your rod and your shepherd’s staff comfort me.  
**23:5** You prepare a ·meal [table] for me in ·front [the presence] of my enemies. You ·pour oil of blessing on my head [anoint my head with oil; oil was a means of refreshment in a hot, dry environment]; you ·fill my cup to overflowing [make my cup overflow; a cup of blessing].  
**23:6** Surely your goodness and ·love [loyalty; mercy] will ·be with [pursue; follow] me all my life, and I will live in the house of the Lord ·forever [for length of days].  

## 24장

**24:1** A Welcome for God into the Temple A psalm of David. The earth belongs to the Lord, and ·everything in it [its fullness]— the world and all its ·people [inhabitants].  
**24:2** He ·built [founded] it on the waters and ·set [established] it on the rivers.  
**24:3** Who may go up on the mountain of the Lord [Zion, the location of the Temple]? Who may stand in his holy ·Temple [place]?  
**24:4** Only those with clean hands and pure hearts [innocent in actions and thoughts], who have not ·worshiped idols [lifted their souls to false things], who have not made promises ·in the name of a false god [or deceitfully].  
**24:5** They will receive a blessing from the Lord; the God who ·saves [rescues; delivers] them will ·declare them right [vindicate them].  
**24:6** ·They try to follow God [This is the generation/people of those who seek him]; they ·look to the God of Jacob for help [search for your face, O God of Jacob]. ·Selah [Interlude]  
**24:7** ·Open up [Lift up your heads], you gates. ·Open wide [Be lifted up], you ·aged [ancient] doors and the ·glorious King [King of glory] will come in.  
**24:8** Who is this ·glorious King [King of glory]? The Lord, strong and mighty. The Lord, ·the powerful warrior [mighty in battle].  
**24:9** ·Open up [Lift up your heads], you gates. ·Open wide [Be lifted up], you ·aged [ancient] doors and the ·glorious King [King of glory] will come in.  
**24:10** Who is this ·glorious King [King of glory]? The Lord ·All-Powerful [of Heaven’s Armies/Hosts]— he is the ·glorious King [King of glory]. ·Selah [Interlude]  

## 25장

**25:1** A Prayer for God to Guide Of David. Lord, I ·give myself [lift my soul] to you;  
**25:2** my God, I ·trust [have confidence in] you. Do not let me be ·disgraced [shamed]; do not let my enemies ·laugh at [triumph/exult over] me.  
**25:3** No one who ·trusts [hopes in; waits on] you will be ·disgraced [shamed], but those who ·sin [betray; are treacherous] without excuse will be ·disgraced [shamed].  
**25:4** Lord, ·tell me [make me know] your ways. ·Show [Teach] me ·how to live [your paths].  
**25:5** ·Guide [Lead] me in your truth, and teach me, my God, my ·Savior [Helper; Victor]. I ·trust [hope in; wait on] you all day long.  
**25:6** Lord, remember your ·mercy [compassion] and ·love [loyalty; covenant love] that you have shown since long ago.  
**25:7** Do not remember the sins and ·wrong things I did when I was young [transgressions of my youth]. But remember ·to love me [according to your love/loyalty/covenant love] always ·because you are good [on account of your goodness], Lord.  
**25:8** The Lord is good and ·right [upright; virtuous]; he ·points [instructs] sinners to the right way.  
**25:9** He shows those who are humble how to do right, and he teaches them his ways.  
**25:10** All the Lord’s ·ways [paths] are ·loving [loyal] and ·true [reliable] for those who ·follow [keep; guard] the demands of his ·agreement [covenant].  
**25:11** For the sake of your ·name [reputation], Lord, forgive my many sins.  
**25:12** ·Are there [Who are…?] those who ·respect [fear] the Lord ? He will ·point [teach] them ·to the best way [the way they should choose].  
**25:13** ·They will enjoy a good life [Their soul will dwell/lodge in goodness], and their ·children [seed] will inherit the ·land [or earth].  
**25:14** The Lord ·tells his secrets to [confides in; or makes friends with] those who ·respect [fear] him; he ·tells them about [makes known to them] his ·agreement [covenant].  
**25:15** My eyes are always ·looking to the Lord for help [toward the Lord]. He will ·keep me [remove my feet] from any traps.  
**25:16** Turn to me and ·have mercy on [be gracious to] me, because I am lonely and hurting.  
**25:17** ·My troubles have [The distress of my heart has] ·grown larger [widened]; ·free me from [bring me out of] my ·problems [anguish; distress].  
**25:18** Look at my ·suffering [affliction] and troubles, and ·take away [forgive] all my sins.  
**25:19** Look at how many enemies I have! See how ·much [violently] they hate me!  
**25:20** Protect me and ·save [rescue; deliver] me. I ·trust [find refuge in] you, so do not let me be ·disgraced [shamed].  
**25:21** My hope is in you, so may ·goodness [blamelessness; innocence] and ·honesty [virtue] guard me.  
**25:22** God, ·save [redeem; ransom] Israel from all their ·troubles [distress]!  

## 26장

**26:1** The Prayer of an Innocent Believer Of David. Lord, ·defend [vindicate] me because I have ·lived an innocent life [walked in innocence]. I have ·trusted [confidence in] the Lord and never ·doubted [wavered; faltered].  
**26:2** Lord, try me and test me; look closely into my ·heart and mind [kidneys and heart; the seat of emotions and mind in Hebrew thought].

**37:3** ·Trust [Have confidence in] the Lord and do good. ·Live [Reside; Settle] in the land and ·feed on truth [or find reliable pastureland].  
**37:4** Enjoy serving the Lord, and he will give you ·what you want [the requests of your heart].  
**37:5** ·Depend on [Commit your way to] the Lord; ·trust [have confidence in] him, and he will take care of you.  
**37:6** Then your ·goodness [righteousness] will shine like the ·sun [light], and your ·fairness [justice] like the noonday sun.  
**37:7** ·Wait [Be quiet before] and ·trust [wait for] the Lord. Don’t be ·upset [worried; angry] ·when others get rich [with the prosperity/success of their way] or when ·someone else’s plans succeed [or they do evil deeds].  
**37:8** ·Don’t get angry [Hold back from anger; Abandon wrath]. Don’t be ·upset [worried; angry]; it only leads to ·trouble [or evil].  
**37:9** Evil people will be ·sent away [cut off], but those who ·trust [wait/pin their hope on] the Lord will inherit the land.  
**37:10** In a little while the wicked will be no more. You may look for them, but they will be ·gone [or no more].  
**37:11** ·People who are not proud [The humble/meek] will inherit the land and will enjoy ·complete peace [or much prosperity].  
**37:12** The wicked make evil plans against ·good [righteous] people. They ·grind [gnash] their teeth at them [in anger].  
**37:13** But the Lord laughs at the wicked, because he sees that their day [of judgment] is coming.  
**37:14** The wicked draw their swords and ·bend [string] their bows to ·kill [fell] the poor and helpless, to ·kill [slaughter] those ·who are honest [whose way is straight].  
**37:15** But their swords will ·stab [enter] their own hearts, and their bows will break.  
**37:16** It is better to have little and be ·right [or righteous] than to have much and be ·wrong [or wicked].  
**37:17** The ·power [arm] of the wicked will be broken, but the Lord ·supports [upholds] those who ·do right [are righteous].  
**37:18** The Lord ·watches over [knows] the ·lives [days] of the ·innocent [blameless], and their ·reward [inheritance] will last forever.  
**37:19** They will not be ashamed ·when trouble comes [in the day of evil/trouble]. They will be ·full [satisfied; satiated] in times of ·hunger [famine].  
**37:20** But the wicked will ·die [perish]. The Lord’s enemies will be like the ·beauty [best] of the ·fields [pastures; flowers or animals]; ·they will disappear [vanishing, they will vanish] ·like [or in] smoke.  
**37:21** The wicked borrow and don’t pay back, but ·those who do right [the righteous] give freely to others.  
**37:22** Those whom ·the Lord [he] blesses will inherit the land, but those he curses will be ·sent away [cut off].  
**37:23** When people’s steps ·follow [are made firm/established by] the Lord, God ·is pleased with [delights in] their ways.  
**37:24** If they stumble, they will not fall, because the Lord ·holds [upholds] their hand.  
**37:25** I was young, and now I am old, but I have never seen ·good [righteous] people ·left helpless [abandoned; forsaken] or their ·children [seed] ·begging for [seeking] food.  
**37:26** Good people always lend freely to others, and their ·children [seed] are a blessing.  
**37:27** ·Stop doing [Turn aside from] evil and do good, so you will ·live [dwell] forever.  
**37:28** The Lord loves ·justice [judgment] and will not ·leave [abandon; forsake] ·those who worship him [his loyal ones/saints]. He will always ·protect [keep; guard] them, but the ·children [seed] of the wicked will ·die [be cut off].  
**37:29** ·Good [Righteous] people will inherit the land and will ·live [dwell] in it forever.  
**37:30** ·Good people speak with [The mouth of the righteous mutters] wisdom, and ·they say what is fair [their tongue speaks justice/judgment].  
**37:31** The ·teachings [instructions; laws] of their God are in their heart, so ·they do not fail to keep them [their steps do not slip/slide/totter].  
**37:32** The wicked watch for ·good [righteous] people ·so that they may [to seek to] kill them.

**37:34** ·Wait for [Hope in] the Lord and ·follow him [keep/guard his way]. He will ·honor [exalt] you and ·give you [you will inherit] the land, and you will see the wicked ·sent away [or destroyed].  
**37:35** I saw a wicked and ·cruel [oppressive] man who ·looked [flourished] like a luxurious cedar tree [strong and healthy].  
**37:36** But he ·died [passed on] and was ·gone [no more]; I ·looked for [sought] him, but he couldn’t be found.  
**37:37** ·Think of [Observe] the ·innocent [blameless] person, and watch the ·honest [upright; virtuous] one. The man who has peace will have ·children to live after him [posterity].

**38:6** I am bent over and bowed down; I ·am sad [walk around mourning] all day long.  
**38:7** ·I am burning with fever [My loins are burned with fever], and my ·whole body [flesh] is ·sore [not sound].  
**38:8** I am weak and ·faint [completely crushed]. I ·moan [groan] from the ·pain I feel [anguish of my heart].  
**38:9** Lord, you know everything I ·want [desire; long for]; my ·cries [sighs] are not hidden from you.  
**38:10** My heart pounds, and my strength ·is gone [abandons/forsakes me]. ·I am losing my sight [The light of my eyes is not with me].

**38:12** ·Some people set traps to kill me [Those who seek my life set traps for me]. Those who ·want [seek] to ·hurt [harm; trouble] me plan trouble; all day long they ·think up [meditate on] ·lies [deception].  
**38:13** I am like the deaf; I cannot hear. Like the mute, I cannot ·speak [open my mouth].  
**38:14** I am like those who do not hear, who have no ·answer [reproof] ·to give [in their mouth].  
**38:15** I ·trust [hope in; wait for] you, Lord. You will answer, my Lord and God.  
**38:16** I said, “Don’t let them ·laugh at [rejoice in] me or ·brag [boast] when ·I am defeated [my foot totters/slips].”  
**38:17** I am ·about to die [ready to stumble], and ·I cannot forget my pain [my pain is constantly with me].  
**38:18** I confess my guilt; I am troubled by my sin.  
**38:19** My ·enemies are strong and [living enemies are] healthy, and many hate me for no reason.  
**38:20** They repay me with evil for the good I did. They ·lie about me [are my accusers/adversaries] because I ·try to do [pursued] good.  
**38:21** Lord, don’t ·leave [abandon; forsake] me; my God, don’t ·go away [be far from me].  
**38:22** Quickly come and help me, my Lord and Savior.

**39:12** “Lord, hear my prayer, and listen to my cry. Do not ignore my tears. I am like a ·visitor [sojourner; wanderer; resident alien] with you. Like my ·ancestors [fathers], I’m only ·here a short time [a temporary resident].  
**39:13** Leave me alone so I can be happy before I leave and am no more.”

## 101장

## 102장

**102:17** He will answer the prayers of the ·needy [lowly; naked]; he will not ·reject [despise] their prayers.

**102:22** People will ·come [gather] together, and kingdoms will serve the Lord.  
**102:23** ·God has made me tired of living [He broke my strength in midcourse/the way]; he has cut short my ·life [days].

**102:27** But you ·never change [are the same/he], and your ·life [years] will never end.  
**102:28** ·Our children [The children of your servants] will live in your presence, and their ·children [offspring; seed] will remain with you.”

**103:17** But the Lord’s ·love [loyalty] for those who ·respect [fear] him continues forever and ever, and his ·goodness [righteousness] continues to their ·grandchildren [children’s children]

**104:21** The lions roar ·as they attack [for prey]. They ·look to [seek] God for food.
